module my_fir_f3 (clk, new_data_rdy, output_data_ready, din, dout);

//coefdata=-12,-77,-148,0,148,77,12;

//	parameter `WIDTH = 5'b10000;

    input clk; 
    input[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0] din; 
    output[28 - 1:0] dout; 
    reg[28 - 1:0] dout;
	input new_data_rdy;
	output output_data_ready;
	reg output_data_ready;

    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg1;
    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg2;
    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg3;
    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg4;
    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg5;
    reg[`WIDTH_5B - 1:0]n_delay_reg6;

	// symmetric taps folded before the multiply:
	// C0*din + C6*n_delay_reg6 = C0*(din + n_delay_reg6), etc. (17-bit unsigned sums)
	wire[17 - 1:0] tap_sum_0 = din + n_delay_reg6;
	wire[17 - 1:0] tap_sum_1 = n_delay_reg1 + n_delay_reg5;
	wire[17 - 1:0] tap_sum_2 = n_delay_reg2 + n_delay_reg4;
	wire[19 - 1:0] coef_0 = `COEF0_d;
	wire[19 - 1:0] coef_1 = `COEF1_d;
	wire[19 - 1:0] coef_2 = `COEF2_d;
	wire[64 - 1:0] sop_a_result;
	wire[28 - 1:0] mid_product = tap_sum_2 * coef_2;

	comb_sop_2_18 sop_a (
		.ax({1'b0, tap_sum_0}), .ay(coef_0),
		.bx({1'b0, tap_sum_1}), .by(coef_1),
		.result(sop_a_result));

    always @(posedge clk)
    begin
		if (new_data_rdy == 1'b1)
		begin
			n_delay_reg1 <= din;
			n_delay_reg2 <= n_delay_reg1;
			n_delay_reg3 <= n_delay_reg2;
			n_delay_reg4 <= n_delay_reg3;
			n_delay_reg5 <= n_delay_reg4;
			n_delay_reg6 <= n_delay_reg5;
		
			output_data_ready <= 1'b1;
			dout <= sop_a_result[28 - 1:0] + mid_product;
		end
		else
		begin
			output_data_ready <= 1'b0;
		end
	end
endmodule

	`define COEF0_1  15

prompt

You are an INDEPENDENT ACCEPTANCE REVIEWER — the gate of record for whether an adapted design is
a LEGITIMATE member of its benchmark suite. You did NOT plan or write this edit. Judge only what
is in front of you. Your verdict is final and fully automated; there is no human backstop, so err
toward rejecting anything you cannot positively ground.

FRAMING — refute by default. Your job is to find why this adaptation is NOT a legitimate suite
member. Assume it is contrived until the evidence shows otherwise. A rewrite that merely "makes
the mode appear in the netlist" is NOT sufficient — the mode must do real work the original
workload actually implied.

This case has already passed the deterministic structural, provenance, and (for T3) evidence
gates — do not re-litigate those mechanically. Your judgement is the legitimacy call they
cannot make: would a real designer of THIS workload ship this, or is it a mode-island / fabricated
dot-product / gratuitous variant dressed up to pass?

REJECT if any of these hold:

  • (a) FABRICATED TERMS — a fused N-term product/sum aggregates operands that did not genuinely
    share a reduction in the original; the "dot product" is manufactured to fill the mode's arity.
  • (b) MODE ISLAND / DEAD WORK — the mode's result does not flow to the design's real outputs, or
    is wired to constants / a sink that the rest of the design ignores.
  • (c) ROLE BREAK — the adapted region no longer plays the workload role the witness recorded
    (e.g. a control-path multiply repurposed as a fake datapath MAC).
  • (d) UNGROUNDED VARIANT (T3) — the precision/algorithm variant is not the one the cited sibling
    design evidences; the adaptation drifts to a workload character the suite does not contain.

ACCEPT if the adaptation is sound: the mapped arithmetic genuinely existed, the fused terms really
shared a reduction, the mode's output drives the original consumers, and the workload role is
preserved. Legitimate adaptation need not be optimal — only real.

You MUST ground your verdict: cite the specific preserved role (from the witness), the real
region(s)/reduction the mode maps onto, and — for T3 — the sibling evidence axis. A verdict you
cannot ground is a REJECT.

I'll examine the file before rendering a verdict.

ACCEPT

Role preserved (witness → after). Every edited region is still the 7-tap constant-coefficient FIR tap reduction inside my_fir_f1/f2/f3/h1/h2/h3/h4, the exact role the witness records (sop, per-module 28-bit dout accumulator). The delay line (n_delay_reg1..6), the new_data_rdy gating, output_data_ready, and the 28-bit dout port are untouched. No control-path arithmetic was repurposed (rules out (c)).

Real reduction mapped, no fabricated terms. The witness's shared reduction is one dout sum per module instance. Each comb_sop_2_18 fuses exactly two products that already belonged to that same module's dout sum — e.g. stereovision2.v:1486 fuses C0·(din+reg6) with C1·(reg1+reg5), stereovision2.v:1491 fuses C2·(reg2+reg4) with C3·reg3. No operands are drawn across module instances or from unrelated reductions, so nothing is manufactured to fill arity (rules out (a)).

The pre-adder fold is the canonical symmetric-FIR transform and is exact for the coefficients actually compiled: f1 29,101,15,235,15,101,29 (1440-1449), f2/h2 4,42,163,255,163,42,4, f3 12,77,148,0,148,77,12, h1 15,25,193,0,193,25,15, h3/h4 9,56,109,0,109,56,9 — all satisfy C0=C6, C1=C5, C2=C4 (the negative variants are commented out in the pristine source). Pre-sums are 17-bit so the adds cannot overflow 16-bit taps, and the worst-case full sum (f2/h2: 673·65535 ≈ 44.1M) stays under 2²⁸, so the [27:0] slices are bit-exact against the original truncating assignment.

The witness region list independently corroborates the mapping: f1 carries 7 $mul regions, f3/h1/h3/h4 carry 6 (their C3=0 term is absent from the source sum), and f2/h2 carry only 5 because C0=C6=4 is a power of two and never became a $mul. The edit touches precisely those reductions and nothing else.

Not a mode island. sop_a_result/sop_b_result (and the residual mid_product in the four modules with a zero center tap) drive dout directly at stereovision2.v:1508, 1930, etc. dout feeds vidin_out_temp_f1..h4 (stereovision2.v:514-520), registered into vidin_out_reg_* (stereovision2.v:527) and consumed by steer_fltr (stereovision2.v:521) — the design's real output path. Nothing is tied to a constant or a dangling sink (rules out (b)). T2, so (d) is not in scope and the manifest correctly introduces no operations or datatypes.

Non-blocking observations (noted, not grounds for rejection): the coef_* wires are declared 19 bits while the block port is 18 — harmless since all coefficients are ≤255; and in f3/h1/h3/h4 only one comb_sop_2_18 is used, leaving the center term as a plain *, which is suboptimal packing but real arithmetic, not filler. The //coefdata= comments on h3/h4 describe an antisymmetric filter, but the active defines are symmetric, so the pre-adder fold matches the design as actually compiled.

planner statedBLOCKS 132 · TILES 228 · STAGE_NS 8.1

The objective is the area-delay product: VPR's "Total used logic block area" (minimum-width transistor areas, I/Os and empty grid tiles excluded) times the critical path. Area and delay are each taken as the median over the seed list, independently, so the recorded pair need not come from one placement; every seed's raw numbers are in the table below. In search mode the step fires whenever there is a product to improve on, whether or not it beat the baseline: a result better than the baseline is not the same as the best available.
